TOTAL_TIME = 60;\n\nconst getRandomWordIndex = (currentIndex: number, total: number): number => {\n  if (total <= 1) return 0;\n  let next = Math.floor(Math.random() * total);\n  while (next === currentIndex) {\n    next = Math.floor(Math.random() * total);\n  }\n  return next;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [gameStatus, setGameStatus] = useState<GameStatus>('idle');\n  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState<number>(TOTAL_TIME);\n  const [currentWordIndex, setCurrentWordIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [charIndex, setCharIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [correctChars, setCorrectChars] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [missCount, setMissCount] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [totalKeystrokes, setTotalKeystrokes] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [completedWords, setCompletedWords] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [activeKey, setActiveKey] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const keyTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  const currentWord: TypingWord = WORDS[currentWordIndex] || WORDS[0];\n\n  // タイマー停止ヘルパー\n  const stopTimer = useCallback(() => {\n    if (timerRef.current !== null) {\n      clearInterval(timerRef.current);\n      timerRef.current = null;\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // ゲーム終了\n  const finishGame = useCallback(() => {\n    stopTimer();\n    setGameStatus('finished');\n  }, [stopTimer]);\n\n  // ゲームスタート\n  const startGame = useCallback(() => {\n    stopTimer();\n    const initialIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * WORDS.length);\n    setCurrentWordIndex(initialIndex);\n    setCharIndex(0);\n    setCorrectChars(0);\n    setMissCount(0);\n    setTotalKeystrokes(0);\n    setCompletedWords(0);\n    setTimeLeft(TOTAL_TIME);\n    setGameStatus('playing');\n\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  }, [stopTimer]);\n\n  // カウントダウンタイマー管理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (gameStatus === 'playing') {\n      timerRef.current = window.setInterval(() => {\n        setTimeLeft((prev) => {\n          if (prev <= 1) {\n            finishGame();\n            return 0;\n          }\n          return prev - 1;\n        });\n      }, 1000);\n    }\n    return () => {\n      stopTimer();\n    };\n  }, [gameStatus, finishGame, stopTimer]);\n\n  // キーハイライトのアニメーション\n  const triggerKeyHighlight = useCallback((key: string) => {\n    setActiveKey(key);\n    if (keyTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n      clearTimeout(keyTimeoutRef.current);\n    }\n    keyTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setActiveKey(null);\n    }, 120);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 打鍵入力ハンドラ\n  const handleKeyAction = useCallback(\n    (inputChar: string) => {\n      if (gameStatus !== 'playing') {\n        if (gameStatus === 'idle') {\n          startGame();\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      triggerKeyHighlight(inputChar);\n      setTotalKeystrokes((prev) => prev + 1);\n\n      const targetChar = currentWord.romaji[charIndex];\n      if (!targetChar) return;\n\n      if (inputChar.toLowerCase() === targetChar.toLowerCase()) {\n        const nextCorrect = correctChars + 1;\n        setCorrectChars(nextCorrect);\n\n        const nextCharIndex = charIndex + 1;\n        if (nextCharIndex >= currentWord.romaji.length) {\n          // 単語クリア\n setCharIndex(nextCharIndex);\n        }\n      } else {\n        // ミス打鍵\n        setMissCount((prev) => prev + 1);\n      }\n    },\n    [gameStatus, currentWord, charIndex, correctChars, startGame, triggerKeyHighlight]\n  );\n\n  // 物理キーボードイベント\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      // 修飾キー単体押しは無視\n      if (['Shift', 'Control', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'Tab', 'CapsLock'].includes(e.key)) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 未開始時にSpaceキーまたはEnterキーで開始\n      if (gameStatus === 'idle' && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter')) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        startGame();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (gameStatus === 'playing') {\n        // 1文字のアルファベットまたは記号\n        if (e.key.length === 1) {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          handleKeyAction(e.key);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [gameStatus, handleKeyAction, startGame]);\n\n  // 入力カードクリックでフォーカス維持\n  const handleCardClick = () => {\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  };\n\n  // 正確率の計算\n  const accuracy =\n    totalKeystrokes > 0\n      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((correctChars / totalKeystrokes) * 100)))\n      : 100;\n\n  // WPMの計算\n  const elapsedMinutes = (TOTAL_TIME - timeLeft) / 60;\n  const wpm =\n    elapsedMinutes > 0 ? Math.round(correctChars / 5 / elapsedMinutes) : 0;\n\n  const stats: TypingStats = {\n    correctChars,\n    totalKeystrokes,\n    missCount,\n    completedWords,\n    wpm,\n    accuracy,\n  };\n\n  // ローマ字の分割表示\n  const typedPart = currentWord.romaji.slice(0, charIndex);\n  const currentChar = currentWord.romaji.slice(charIndex, charIndex + 1);\n  const untypedPart = currentWord.romaji.slice(charIndex + 1);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"typing-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"header-title\">60秒ローマ字タイピング</h1>\n        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">60秒間で何文字打てるか限界に挑戦しよう！</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"stats-bar\" aria-label=\"ゲーム状況\">\n        <div className=\"stat-badge stat-badge-time\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">残り時間</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{timeLeft}秒</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat-badge\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">打てた文字数</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{correctChars}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat-badge\">\n          <span <span className=\"romaji-untyped\">{untypedPart}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"typing-input-wrapper\">\n          <input\n            ref={inputRef}\n            type=\"text\"\n            className=\"hidden-input\"\n            aria-label=\"タイピング入力欄\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            autoCorrect=\"off\"\n            autoCapitalize=\"off\"\n 0}</span>\n          <span className=\"result-hero-unit\">文字 / 60秒</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"result-grid\">\n          <div className=\"result-item\">\n            <span className=\"result-item-label\">入力速度 (WPM)</span>\n            <span className=\"result-item-value\">{stats?.wpm ?? TOTAL_TIME = 60;\n\nconst getRandomWordIndex = (currentIndex: number, total: number): number => {\n  if (total <= 1) return 0;\n  let next = Math.floor(Math.random() * total);\n  while (next === currentIndex) {\n    next = Math.floor(Math.random() * total);\n  }\n  return next;\n};\n\n// Web Audio API による効果音再生\nconst playSound = (type: 'type' | 'miss' | 'complete') => {\n  try {\n    const AudioCtx =\n      window.AudioContext ||\n      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n    if (!AudioCtx) return;\n    const ctx = new AudioCtx();\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const gain = ctx.createGain();\n    osc.connect(gain);\n    gain.connect(ctx.destination);\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    if (type === 'type') {\n      osc.type = 'sine';\n      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(600, now);\n      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, now);\n      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.05);\n      osc.start(now);\n      osc.stop(now + 0.05);\n    } else if (type === 'miss') {\n      osc.type = 'sawtooth';\n      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(160, now);\n      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, now);\n      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.16);\n      osc.start(now);\n      osc.stop(now + 0.16);\n    } else if (type === 'complete') {\n      osc.type = 'triangle';\n null>(null);\n  const keyTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const missTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  const currentWord: TypingWord = WORDS[currentWordIndex] || WORDS[0];\n\n  // タイマー停止ヘルパー\n  const stopTimer = useCallback(() => {\n    if (timerRef.current !== null) {\n      clearInterval(timerRef.current);\n      timerRef.current = null;\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // ゲーム終了\n  const finishGame = useCallback(() => {\n    stopTimer();\n    setGameStatus('finished');\n  }, [stopTimer]);\n\n  // 次の単語を選択してリセット\n  const selectNextWord = useCallback((currentIndex: number) => {\n setCharIndex(0);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ゲームスタート\n  const startGame = useCallback(() => {\n    stopTimer();\n    const initialIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * WORDS.length);\n    const initialWord = WORDS[initialIndex];\n    setCurrentWordIndex(initialIndex);\n    setCurrentRomaji(initialWord.romaji);\n    setCharIndex(0);\n    setCorrectChars(0);\n    setMissCount(0);\n    setTotalKeystrokes(0);\n    setCompletedWords(0);\n    setTimeLeft(TOTAL_TIME);\n    setIsMissed(false);\n    setGameStatus('playing');\n\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  }, [stopTimer]);\n\n  // カウントダウンタイマー管理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (gameStatus === 'playing') {\n      timerRef.current = window.setInterval(() => {\n useCallback((key: string) => {\n    setActiveKey(key);\n    if (keyTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n      clearTimeout(keyTimeoutRef.current);\n    }\n    keyTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setActiveKey(null);\n    }, 120);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ミス発生時のアニメーションと効果音\n  const triggerMissFeedback = useCallback(() => {\n    setMissCount((prev) => prev + 1);\n    setIsMissed(true);\n    playSound('miss');\n\n    if (missTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n      clearTimeout(missTimeoutRef.current);\n    }\n    missTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsMissed(false);\n    }, 280);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 打鍵入力ハンドラ（代替ローマ字対応）\n  const handleKeyAction = useCallback(\n    (inputChar: string) => {\n      if (gameStatus !== 'playing') {\n        if (gameStatus === 'idle') {\n          startGame();\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      triggerKeyHighlight(inputChar);\n      setTotalKeystrokes((prev) => prev + 1);\n\n      // 利用可能なローマ字候補（標準表記 + 代替表記）\n      const candidates = [currentWord.romaji, ...(currentWord.alternativeRomaji || [])];\n      const typedPrefix = currentRomaji.slice(0, charIndex);\n      const expectedPrefix = (typedPrefix + inputChar).toLowerCase();\n\n      // 現在の入力文字列と前方一致するローマ字表記を検索\n      const matchedCandidate = candidates.find((cand) =>\n        cand.toLowerCase().startsWith(expectedPrefix)\n      );\n\n      if (matchedCandidate) {\n        // マッチした候補表記を採用して反映\n        setCurrentRomaji(matchedCandidate);\n        const nextCorrect = correctChars + 1;\n        setCorrectChars(nextCorrect);\n\n        const nextCharIndex = charIndex + 1;\n        if (nextCharIndex >= matchedCandidate.length) {\n          // 単語クリア\n          setCompletedWords((prev) => prev + 1);\n          playSound('complete');\n          selectNextWord(currentWordIndex);\n        } else {\n          setCharIndex(nextCharIndex);\n          playSound('type');\n        }\n      } else {\n        // ミス打鍵\n        triggerMissFeedback();\n      }\n    },\n    [\n      gameStatus,\n      currentWord,\n      currentRomaji,\n      charIndex,\n      correctChars,\n      currentWordIndex,\n      startGame,\n      triggerKeyHighlight,\n      triggerMissFeedback,\n      selectNextWord,\n    ]\n  );\n\n  // 物理キーボードイベント\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      // 修飾キー単体押しは無視\n      if (['Shift', 'Control', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'Tab', 'CapsLock'].includes(e.key)) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 未開始時または結果画面表示時にSpaceまたはEnterキーで開始・リトライ\n      if ((gameStatus === 'idle' || gameStatus === 'finished') && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter')) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        startGame();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (gameStatus === 'playing') {\n        // 1文字のアルファベットまたはハイフン等の記号\n        if (e.key.length === 1) {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          handleKeyAction(e.key);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [gameStatus, handleKeyAction, startGame]);\n\n  // 入力カードクリックでフォーカス維持\n  const handleCardClick = () => {\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  };\n\n  // 正確率の計算\n  const accuracy =\n    totalKeystrokes > 0\n      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round((correctChars / totalKeystrokes) * 100)))\n      : 100;\n\n  // WPMの計算\n  const elapsedMinutes = (TOTAL_TIME - timeLeft) / 60;\n  const wpm =\n    elapsedMinutes > 0 ? Math.round(correctChars / 5 / elapsedMinutes) : 0;\n\n  const stats: TypingStats = {\n    correctChars,\n    totalKeystrokes,\n    missCount,\n    completedWords,\n    wpm,\n    accuracy,\n  };\n\n  // 現在のローマ字の分割表示\n  const typedPart = currentRomaji.slice(0, charIndex);\n  const currentChar = currentRomaji.slice(charIndex, charIndex + 1);\n  const untypedPart = currentRomaji.slice(charIndex + 1);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"typing-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"header-title\">60秒ローマ字タイピング</h1>\n        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">60秒間で何文字打てるか限界に挑戦しよう！</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"stats-bar\" aria-label=\"ゲーム状況\">\n        <div className=\"stat-badge stat-badge-time\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">残り時間</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{timeLeft}秒</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat-badge\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">打てた文字数</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{correctChars}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat-badge\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">正確率</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{accuracy}%</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat-badge\">\n          <span className=\"stat-label\">ミス数</span>\n          <span className=\"stat-value\">{missCount}</span>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <main\n        className={`game-card ${isMissed ? 'shake-error' : ''}`.trim()}\n        onClick={handleCardClick}\n      >\n        <div className=\"prompt-container\">\n          <div className=\"prompt-kanji\">{currentWord.kanji}</div>\n          <div className=\"prompt-kana\">{currentWord.kana}</div>\n          <div className=\"prompt-romaji\" aria-label=\"ローマ字ガイド\">\n            <span className=\"romaji-typed\">{typedPart}</span>\n            <span className=\"romaji-current\">{currentChar}</span>\n            <span className=\"romaji-untyped\">{untypedPart}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"typing-input-wrapper\">\n          <input\n            ref={inputRef}\n            type=\"text\"\n            className=\"hidden-input\"\n            aria-label=\"タイピング入力欄\"\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            autoCorrect=\"off\"\n            autoCapitalize=\"off\"\n            spellCheck=\"false\"\n            value=\"\"\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              const val = e.target.value;\n              if (val.length > 0) {\n                handleKeyAction(val[val.length - 1]);\n              }\n            }}\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        {gameStatus === 'idle' && (\n          <div className=\"mobile-input-helper\">\n            スペースキーまたは下の「スタート」ボタンで計測を開始\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </main>\n\n      <section className=\"controls-container\">\n        {gameStatus === 'idle' && (\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={startGame}>\n            スタート（60秒計測）\n          </button>\n        )}\n        {gameStatus === 'playing' && (\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn-secondary\" onClick={startGame}>\n            リセット\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      <VirtualKeyboard\n        activeKey={activeKey}\n        targetKey={currentChar}\n        onKeyPress={handleKeyAction}\n      />\n\n      {gameStatus === 'finished' && (\n        <ResultModal stats={stats} onRestart={startGame} />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}
